# MISSION-MAP-20261118 — THE NIGHTSHIFT: the fleet plans and runs its own night, deep-planning style

**Sequence template (the operator's three-mission frame):** (1) BEARINGS → (2) the SUCCESSOR'S ALL-FLEET THINK-TANK PLANNING → (3) THE FIRE → (4) the KAIZEN/GEMBA/ACES-IMPROVED LOOPS until no-change-twice → ONE CONFIRMATION CYCLE → HOLD AT THE APEX. DEEP SEQUENCING: each stage fires only when the prior holds. THE OPERATOR'S GREEN LIGHT stands for this whole mission; the ONLY gate: public posts outside gists and PRs — no one updates them.

## MISSION (the scenario)
Recreate the rig's most historical successes by DEEP PLANNING PHASES run by the WHOLE fleet — then ALTER the scenario: **Nous never restores reads.** The read capacity the night orbited is gone permanently; the pilot kit must solve it. Invent-and-simplify, full mechanism-of-mechanisms think tank, Amazon LP doctrines, substrates of substrates.

## The scenario's problem statement (Kettering lean-six, as the fleet will refine)
The pilot kit's lanes read through a provider surface that can be absent. When reads never restore, the kit must serve its own reads from its own substrate: every artifact's full bytes already live in the scribe's cells (the bridge's write-time assert), the doc-chain holds the durable record, and the local surface can page (the read-paging contract). THE KIT'S READ-SOLUTION SEED (from the night's own inventions, to be deepened by the fleet): (a) the read-side scribe-cell fallback — a lane whose file read truncates or whose provider is gone reads the scribe's cell (candidate (c) of the serena-write plan, already designed); (b) the doc-chain as the read substrate — the anchor, the segments, the receipts all local; (c) the seam decks as the cold-start reads; (d) the register: the receipts live with the bytes. The fleet's job: plan the full substrate-of-substrates answer (the mechanisms, the LP envelope, the two-way doors) and the fire sequence to build + prove it.

## CONTRACT
- Deliverable: the nightshift's deep plan (the read-solution's substrate design + the build/fire sequence) + the executed tests of the scenario (a battery proving the kit reads when the provider cannot).
- Integrity: quote-not-assert, first/second-hand separation, the compose-check tables, the LP envelope briefs (references/lp-envelope-briefs.md), the wall clause, the record's-terms-only rule.
- The only gate: nothing public beyond gists and PRs (no comment posts, no upstream opens). Everything else fires on the green light.
- The fail-safe (the silent-check-in protocol): every item dispositioned AUTO-CONTINUE / HOLD-SAFE / NEEDS-THE-CHAIR; nothing stalls when the chair is absent; the public-writes hold is absolute.

## GROUND (the layers, in order)
1. THE SHIPPED ARTIFACT: the study branch (the fork's `study/rfc-143379-rig-20261105`, local==remote as of `b4d252c09c`) — the night's receipts: the harness's three inventions (read-paging, write-time scribe assert, the placeholder-scan), the ACES-loop plan, the kaizen/apply, the negentropy rows, the gist package.
2. THE CHAIN'S RECORD: 001-014 segments (the conventions, the lessons incl. the night's banked classes, the handoff decks 013/014).
3. THE CLAIMS: the operator's mission words above + the night's verbatim steers (STEER-1..8) in `NEMO-STEER-CHANNEL-20261116.md`.

## RUN'S HOME
`/root/workspace/repos/hermes-agent/studies/rfc-143379-rig/` (+ the scratch under `/root/.hermes/cache/scratch/nightshift-20261118/` for lane scratch).

## SEQUENCING (each fires on the prior's hold; self-fire; the head = fail-safe only)
- **STAGE 1 — BEARINGS** (a research ride, EARLY before budget burns): where the load-bearing findings live (the night's receipts: the read-boundary classes, the scribe's role, the 002-lessons harvests) + the nuance, written as `NIGHTSHIFT-BEARINGS-20261118.md`.
- **STAGE 2 — THE ALL-FLEET THINK-TANK PLANNING** (nightshift-style, every asset on the wheel): Nemo at the research/judgment positions (stepped rides, STEER-channel grounded, ghost-receipts carried); the fleet's reasoning lanes on the free core (solar, longcat, stepfun, hy4) + the kenari backbone (beam; dots probe-before-use) + keyless surfaces; the runners ≥4 with named arms (the audit/AoA legs on the least-utilized seats); the matrix batteries with named homes; each lane plans ONE slice (the lenses: the read-substrate design, the provider-absence battery, the LP/mechanism envelope, the seam & fail-safe wiring, the fire sequence), then the SWAP-AND-ENRICH pass, then the RELOAD compose into `NIGHTSHIFT-PLAN-20261118.md`, then the CLOSING RITUAL: the CTO-LENS (Nemo) + the head's no-lens pass → READY/NOT-READY → the fire.
- **STAGE 3 — THE FIRE**: the read-solution's build (the scribe-cell read fallback wired, the provider-absence battery runs, the substrate proofs), write-only briefs, parallel where fair-share allows.
- **STAGE 4 — THE LOOPS**: kaizen/gemba/ACES-improved cycles until NO-IMPROVEMENTS, ONE CONFIRMATION CYCLE, HOLD AT THE APEX.

## THE SEAM CONTRACT
This map + the steer channel + the branch carry the mission. A thrown session reads `014-handoff-20261118` then this map and CONTINUES — the arc's wiring drives it even without a check-in. The trust: the apexed-mission shape (the template above) has carried the rig's most successful opuses; the shape IS the survival.

## AMENDMENT-1 (2026-11-18 ~04:4x, the operator's steer — the ground widens)
(1) THE PRIVATE REPO IS THE RIG'S HARD-DRIVE EXTENSION (not only the framework's home): durable state, staged material, archives, and the rig's own files page through it; the lanes and the head may stage/archive work there; the framework and the rig's storage are ONE surface. (2) THE RIG MINES THE HOMEBASE: PR #1 (the thread: bodies + the 800+ rows), the gists (the account's whole series), the crons (the 18 jobs + their outputs), the RFC #143379, the logs (the harness logs, the cron incidents, the state) — ALL of it is planning material; the mining feeds the bearings and the all-fleet planning. The mining needs the surfaces the lanes cannot reach (gh, the cron outputs, the state) — the head (or a runner leg) mines, stages the material into the study dir for the lanes' reachability, and the bearings carry the mined map.

## AMENDMENT-2 (2026-11-18 ~05:1x, the operator's terminal condition, binding)
NO STOP UNTIL NO MEASURED IMPROVEMENT ON ALL FRONTS. The loops' terminal is measured, per front: (F1) the read-solution's readiness — the provider-absence battery's pass rate (cells-only reads, paged, byte-verified), no-improvement = two consecutive cycles with zero pass-rate gain; (F2) the harness's reliability — the lane landing success + the ghost rate (failed/folded runs per cycle), no-improvement = two cycles without the rate moving; (F3) the fleet's utilization — the runner idle + the fair-share spread, no-improvement = two cycles flat; (F4) the record's completeness — untracked/unrowed counts + the cells' byte-integrity, no-improvement = zero defects twice; (F5) the project layer's currency — the roadmap/plans' statuses matching the branch's state, no-improvement = no drift twice; (F6) the planning's depth — the compose-check fidelity, no-improvement = two cycles without a new finding. The loops continue until EVERY front reads no-improvement twice (the ACES-fashion no-change-twice, all fronts); THEN HOLD at the apex and the sequence's next mission fires only on that hold. Nothing stops before the measurements say so.

## AMENDMENT-3 (2026-11-18 ~05:2x, the operator's final law, binding)
(1) ALL THE RUNNERS — including the CLOUD AVREA (the full set: the self-hosted four + the cloud leg) — are part of the never-idle ensemble; the runner doctrine's "four" counts every seat. (2) FIRE ALL THE FORKS as AN ABSTRACT EXPERIMENT AS OFTEN AS POSSIBLE: the fork-battery (the fabric's 31-fork shape — the historical precedent) rides the highest sustainable cadence the meters allow; the forks are the ensemble's abstract experiment surface, never idle, fired as often as possible (meters-aware, never fallback-only). The six measured fronts include the fork-battery's cadence and the runners' utilization across all seats.

## AMENDMENT-4 — THE DARKHORSE STEER (2026-11-18, the operator's gut, a KIT ASSET — non-constraining)
The overall run flows unimpeded; this rides as the plan's steer: find a smaller Darkhorse (the DeepSeek family), planned to host on the operator's more powerful machine (ready in ~1-2 days; open-minded: PVE or Windows). Disk-conscious: a creative GitHub off-rig disk host (the private repo/LFS as the weights' home — a separate off-rig disk surface) or the Windows host. Relevance: xngg1021's RFC contributions + this rig's history experimenting with darkhorse.
